Let's think of algorithms for discrete optimization. Previous program got 1.516 using multiplicative threshold. Need different approach to break plateau. Potential ideas:

Need exact score function. Use c = n*c? We'll write:
```
def score_and_arrays(p, L=None):
   n=p.size
   if L is None: L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   F=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0)
   d=n*c
   score=2*d.max()
   return score,d,c,L
```
Use float64.

Softmax from z:
```
def softmax(z):
   z = z - np.max(z)
   e=np.exp(z)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: fallback uniform
   return e/s
```

```
def corr_p_w(p,w,L):
   # want r_m = sum_{i} p_i w_{i+m}, m=0..n-1, w valid m+ i up to2n-2
   # use full fft? length L at least 2n-1? max index 2n-2. L=nextpow2(2n-1)
   P=np.fft.fft(p, L)
   W=np.fft.fft(w, L)
```

Algorithm for one run (n, z):
```
best_z, best_score = z, score(p)
alpha=0.1; eps=0.2; beta=10; stall=0
while time<limit:
  p=softmax(z)
  score,d = eval(p)
  if score<best: best...
  maxd=d.max(); target_d=target/2
  if maxd > target_d:
     tau=target_d + (1-eps)*(maxd-target_d)
  else:
     tau=maxd - 0.05*maxd # keep small
  x=d-tau; w=np.exp(beta*x - max); if sum<... fallback softmax(beta*d)
  A=corr(p,w); B=np.dot(w,d)
  G=n*p*(n*A - B)
  sgn=np.max(abs(G)); if sgn>0: G/=sgn
  # line search
  accepted=False
  for a in [alpha*0.5, alpha, alpha*2] maybe:
      ztry=z - a*G
      ztry-=mean
      ptry=softmax(ztry); sc=score(ptry)
      if sc < current_score - 1e-12:
          accept best among? choose sc min.
  if accepted:
      alpha=best_a*1.1
      stall=0
  else:
      alpha*=0.5; stall++
      if stall>... perturb z += normal*sigma; alpha=...
      if stall>... reset to best or random
  # adjust eps,beta
  if score<target +0.05: eps=min(1,eps*1.01); beta=min(200,beta*1.05)
  else: maybe eps=max(0.05,eps*0.99)
```

Implement main loop as:
```
def run_descent(p_init, n, time_limit, target=1.5027, seed):
   p=sanitize init normalized
   z=None maybe log(p+eps)
   best=score
   theta=0.2; alpha=0.2; beta=10; eps_tau=0.3; stall=0
   while time_left:
     score,d = eval(p)
     maxd=d.max()
     if maxd > targetd:
        tau=targetd + (1-eps_tau)*(maxd-targetd)
     else: tau=0.99*maxd
     x=d-tau; w=exp(beta*x - xmax); if sum<... w=softmax(beta*d)
     A=corr(p,w); B=dot(w,d)
     H=n*A - B; H-=H.mean(); mx=H.max(abs)
     if mx < 1e-12: perturb
     v=H/mx
     # try primary multiplicative with alpha
     zn = log(p+1e-300) - alpha*v; zn-=mean; pn=softmax(zn); sc=score(pn)
     if sc < score:
        p=pn; alpha=min(5, alpha*1.1); stall=0
     else:
        # try additive theta
        q = p - theta*(1/n)*v; q=max(q,0); q/=sum
        sc=score(q)
        if sc<score: accept; ...
        else: reduce alpha,theta; stall++
        if stall>... random/noise/reset
     adjust eps,beta
```

```
def make_weights(d, beta, tau):
    x = d - tau
    x_max = x.max()
    # Instead of hard zero, use soft threshold: x = np.maximum(x, 0) * maybe + small floor for all?
    w = np.exp(beta * x - beta * x_max)
    # To avoid weights on far below, zero where x < -1e-12:
    w[x < 0] = 0.0
    s = w.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
       # fallback soft top: use top 20% quantile
       thresh = np.quantile(d, 0.8)
       x = d-thresh; w=exp...
    else: w/=s
    return w
```

`_score_d` should sanitize p? It can assume sanitized. But for safety, if not finite, return inf. Use local L.
```
def _score_d(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float).ravel()
   n=p.size
   if n==0: return inf, np.array([])
   p=np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1000, neginf=0.0)
   p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0: return inf, ...
   p=p/s
   L,idx=_fft_info(n)
   Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp,L)
   c=c[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   d=n*c
   sc=2.0*float(np.max(d))
   return sc,d
```
If all c zero? p sum1, no.

`_corr_A`:
```
def _corr_A(p,w,L,idx):
   Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L); Fw=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
   A=np.fft.irfft(Fp*np.conj(Fw), L)
   A=A[idx]
   # A should nonnegative; but if tiny negative:
   np.maximum(A,0.0,out=A)
   return A
```
But `np.maximum` alloc? Use `A=np.where(A>0,A,0)`.

`_make_weights`:
```
def _make_weights(d,beta,tau):
   K=d.size
   maxd=float(np.max(d))
   if maxd<=0 or not finite:
       return np.full(K,1.0/K)
   if tau is None or tau < 0: tau=0.5*maxd
   if tau >= maxd:
       # choose top quantile
       tau = maxd - 1e-6*maxd
   x=d-tau
   mask = x > 0.0
   if not np.any(mask):
       # fallback top 30 quantile or softmax all
       q = np.quantile(d, 0.7) if K>1 else 0
       x = d - q
       mask = x > 0
       if not np.any(mask): mask = True; x=d
   x_max = float(x[mask].max())
   w = np.zeros_like(d)
   xuse = beta * (x[mask] - x_max)
   # To avoid underflow, if beta*xuse < -700 okay exp 0
   w[mask] = np.exp(xuse)
   s = float(w.sum())
   if s <= 1e-300 or not finite:
      shift=d.max(); w=np.exp(beta*(d-shift)); s=w.sum(); if fail w uniform
   w/=s
   return w
```
If mask too broad (tau low) and beta small, okay.
Maybe include all coefficients with a tiny floor to avoid B based solely top, but hard zero okay. For direction, if top 10% narrow, mean of nA not exactly? Good.
Potential issue: If d has many equal max (flat), mask d>tau includes all if tau below max. B=max. H=nA-B; mean(nA) maybe around1, B maybe max~0.75; H center positive, etc. Good.

```
def _random_p(n, style, rng):
    t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    if style==0: trig
    elif style==1: sine asym
    elif style==2: piecewise
    elif style==3: smooth noise
    else: sigmoid step
    z=np.asarray...
    z=np.nan_to_num...
    z=np.clip(z,-30,30)
    z=z-np.mean(z)
    p=_softmax(z)
    # small floor? no; _softmax positive.
    return p
```

Pseudo code (no lambda):
```
def _descent(p_init, n, time_limit, target_score, rng, band_start=0.12, beta_start=8.0, alpha_start=0.3, theta_start=0.3, verbose=False):
    p = _sanitize(p_init)
    if p is None or p.size != n: p=_resample(...); if None uniform
    p=p.astype(float); p=p/p.sum()
    best_p=p.copy(); best_sc, d = _score_d(p)
    current_sc=best_sc
    band=band_start; beta=beta_start; alpha=alpha_start; theta=theta_start
    target_d=target_score*0.5
    inv_n=1.0/n
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    stall=0; it=0; last_prev=best_sc
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       # Ensure current score,d; recompute if p changed
       current_sc, d = _score_d(p)
       if current_sc < best_sc: best_sc=current_sc; best_p=p.copy()
       maxd=float(np.max(d))
       if verbose and it%200==0: print(...)
       # direction
       if maxd > target_d:
           # band relative? adapt
           if maxd - target_d > 0.2: band=max(band,0.25)
           tau=max(target_d, maxd - band)
       else:
           tau=max(0.0, maxd - 0.05*maxd) if maxd>0 else 0
       if tau >= maxd: tau=maxd - 1e-6*maxd
       w=_make_weights(d,beta,tau)
       L,idx=_fft_info(n)
       A=_corr_A(p,w,L,idx)
       na=n*A
       mean_na=float(np.mean(na))
       vp=na-mean_na
       B=float(np.dot(w,d))
       vl=na-B
       # normalize
       vmax=float(np.max(np.abs(vp)))
       if vmax<1e-12:
           vp=vl
           vmax=float(np.max(np.abs(vp)))
       if vmax<1e-12:
           # perturb and continue
           z=_z_of_p(p)+rng.normal(0,0.1,n); p=_softmax(z); current_sc,_=...
           stall+=1; continue
       vp=vp/vmax
       vlmax=float(np.max(np.abs(vl)))
       if vlmax<1e-12: vl=vp
       else: vl=vl/vlmax
       improved=False
       # mult candidate with vl
       z=_z_of_p(p)
       zn=z - alpha*vl
       zn=zn - np.mean(zn)
       pn=_softmax(zn)
       sc,_=_score_d(pn)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < current_sc - 1e-12:
           p=pn; current_sc=sc; alpha=min(8.0, alpha*1.15); theta=max(theta*0.95, 0.05)? (don't reduce add)
           improved=True
       # add candidate with vp
       if not improved:
           q=p - theta*inv_n*vp
           s=float(q.sum()) # after clip? q can negative
           q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
           s=float(q.sum())
           if s>0 and np.isfinite(s):
               q=q/s
               sc,_=_score_d(q)
               if sc < current_sc - 1e-12:
                   p=q; current_sc=sc; theta=min(10.0, theta*1.15); improved=True
       if improved:
           if current_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
               best_sc=current_sc; best_p=p.copy()
           stall=0
           # adjust band/beta toward narrower/higher
           band=max(0.02, band*0.97)
           beta=min(120.0, beta*1.01)
       else:
           alpha=max(0.02, alpha*0.65)
           theta=max(0.02, theta*0.65)
           band=min(0.45, band*1.15)
           beta=max(1.0, beta*0.92)
           stall += 1
           if stall >= 6:
               sigma=0.05 + 0.01*min(stall,40)
               z=_z_of_p(p) + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
               pc=_softmax(z)
               sc,_=_score_d(pc)
               # accept if not catastrophic relative to best
               if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= max(best_sc*1.25, current_sc*1.5) and sc < 2.5:
                   p=pc; current_sc=sc
               else:
                   p=best_p.copy(); current_sc=best_sc
               stall=0
               alpha=max(0.1, alpha); theta=max(0.1, theta)
           if stall >= 120: # actually after reset stall=0; use counter reset_count maybe
               pass
       it += 1
    # final maybe local? no
    return best_p, best_sc
```

Now, `_local_block_search` implementation:
```
def _local_block_search(p_init, time_limit, rng, verbose=False):
   p=_sanitize(p_init)
   n=p.size
   if n==0: return p, inf
   best_sc,d=_score_d(p); best_p=p.copy(); current_sc=best_sc
   deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
   stalled=0
   it=0
   small=max(1,n//2048)
   mid=max(small+1,n//256)
   large=max(mid+1,min(n,n//32))
   while time.monotonic()<deadline:
      r=rng.random()
      if r<0.35: w=1
      elif r<0.7: w=int(rng.integers(2, max(3, small*8+1)))
      elif r<0.95: w=int(rng.integers(max(1,mid), max(2, mid*8+1)))
      else: w=int(rng.integers(max(1,large), max(2, large*8+1)))
      w=min(max(1,w),n)
      start=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1)) if n>w else 0
      best_cand=None; best_sc_cur=current_sc
      factors=(0.5,0.7,0.85,1.15,1.3,1.6,2.0,0.9,1.1)
      for f in factors:
          cand=p.copy()
          cand[start:start+w] *= f
          cand=np.where(np.isfinite(cand),cand,0.0)
          cand=np.clip(cand,0,1000)
          s=float(cand.sum())
          if s<=0: continue
          cand/=s
          sc,_=_score_d(cand)
          if sc < best_sc_cur - 1e-12:
              best_sc_cur=sc; best_cand=cand.copy()
      if best_cand is not None:
          p=best_cand; current_sc=best_sc_cur; stalled=0
          if current_sc<best_sc: best_sc=current_sc; best_p=p.copy()
      else:
          stalled+=1
          if stalled>=20:
             # random multiplicative perturbation
             z=_z_of_p(p)+rng.normal(0,0.05,min(0.3,0.05+0.005*stalled),n)
             pc=_softmax(z)
             sc,_=_score_d(pc)
             if sc <= max(best_sc*1.05, current_sc*1.15) and sc<2.2:
                p=pc; current_sc=sc
             else:
                p=best_p.copy(); current_sc=best_sc
             stalled=0
          if stalled>=150: p=best_p.copy(); current=best_sc; stalled=0
      it+=1
   return best_p,best_sc
```
This restarts p to best frequently; okay. Could be slow if 9 factors each iter. Use 6 factors for speed: (0.5,0.75,0.9,1.1,1.3,1.7). 6 evals. Good.
Maybe block search too many evals but final phase.

```
# parent
```

```
def _resample(p,n):
   p=_sanitize(p)
   if p is None: return None
   n=int(n); if n<=0 return None
   m=p.size
   if m==n: return p.copy()
   if m==1: return np.ones(n)/n
   x_old=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m; x_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,p)
   out=np.maximum(out,0)+1e-12
   return _sanitize(out)
```
- `_add_candidate`: pool sort each time. If pool huge > keep, sort and del. Use `_item_key`.
```
def _add_candidate(pool,p,score=None,keep=48):
   p=_sanitize(p)
   if p is None: return
   if score is None or not np.isfinite(score): score,_=_score_d(p)
   if not np.isfinite(score): return
   pool.append((float(score), int(p.size), p.copy()))
   pool.sort(key=_item_key)
   if len(pool)>keep: del pool[keep:]
```

Let's think about a potential mathematical issue: We are minimizing `max np.convolve(a,a)`, which is polynomial square with ordered pairs. Our FFT uses square Fp^2. Good. For real p, coefficients can be larger at center. The derivative formulas for polynomial square: c_k = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1} p_i p_{k-i}. Derivative wrt p_m = p_{k-m} + p_{k-m} if both terms? What if k-m=m? Then only one term? Wait in the sum ∑_{i} p_i p_{k-i}, when i=m, term p_m p_{k-m}. If k-2m≠0 there is no second term because the sum only over i, not over ordered pairs? But convolution of polynomial A(z)^2 coefficient is ∑_{i+j=k} p_i p_j. The formula ∑_{i} p_i p_{k-i} counts ordered pairs because i runs over all, and for i and j swapped both i values if both in range. If i=m and j=k-m, the term appears once at i=m. The swapped term appears at i=j (if j in range), which is another term p_j p_i. If i=m = j (k=2m), they are same; derivative of p_m^2 is 2p_m. Our formula derivative =? Taking derivative of ∑_i p_i p_{k-i}: term i=m derivative p_{k-m} (which equals p_m if k=2m). Also if i=k-m (same if i=m; no second), so derivative only p_{k-m} not 2? Wait when i=m and k=2m, term is p_m p_m; derivative 2p_m. Our expression derivative of term is p_{k-m} + p_m (for u v) = p_m + p_m =2p_m. For distinct, derivative of term i=m is p_{k-m}; term i=k-m is p_m; total2. Thus formula 2p_{k-m} if k-m valid. Good. Thus ∂c_k/∂p_m=2p_{k-m}. Good.
But in weighted sum ∂S/∂p_i=2∑w_k p_{k-i}. This matches A_i=∑w_k p_{k-i}. Good. Note A_i as defined earlier with p index k? We set A_i=Σ_j w_j p_{j-i}; yes. Good.
Cross-correlation A_i =∑_k p_k w_{i+k}? Here j=i+k => p_k w_{i+k}; yes. Good.
Mean of A_i for p-gradient: ∑_i A_i = Σ_j w_j S_j not necessarily 1. We use `mean(na)=np.mean(na)`, correct. H_p should be nA - mean(nA). Good.
For log gradient, B=Σw d_j; gradient wrt z =2p_i(nA_i-B). We use vl=na-B. Good.

In `_descent`, additive candidate using vp=na-mean(na) scaled. The theoretical descent direction for S_w with p variables is `- (G - mean(G))` where G=2nA. Scaling sign: We set q = p - theta v where v=vp (not negative). If projected gradient G-mean = positive at high A, descent p should decrease there, so p - positive is correct. Good.
Mult using vl: negative gradient in z is -2p_i(nA_i-B). If we use z -= alpha*(nA-B), this is in direction of -gradient if p_i positive and alpha positive. Good.
So signs correct.

```
def _random_p(n, style, rng):

   ...
```

Block length random: If n large, `max(2, small*8+1)` as upper for rng integers high exclusive; if high<=low, set high=low+1. Use helper? Inline.
```
def _rand_block_w(n, rng):
   r=rng.random()
   if n <= 64:
      w=int(rng.integers(1,n+1)); return w
   small=max(1,n//2048)
   mid=max(small+1,n//256)
   large=max(mid+1, min(n, n//32))
   if r < 0.35:
      w=1
   elif r<0.7:
      hi=max(small+1, small*8+1)
      w=int(rng.integers(1, hi+1))
   elif r<0.95:
      lo=max(1,mid); hi=max(lo+1, mid*8+1)
      w=int(rng.integers(lo, min(n, hi)+1))
   else:
      lo=max(1,large); hi=max(lo+1, large*8+1)
      w=int(rng.integers(lo, min(n, hi)+1))
   return min(max(1,w), n)
```

```
def _search_size(parent_p, pool, n, t_alloc, rng, target_score, verbose=False):

    # pool best
    if pool:
        idx=0; best=pool[0] # pool sorted? not necessarily after `_search_size`? We keep pool sorted in add. But if not, min.
```

```
def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, seed=12345):
    start=time.monotonic()
    try budget=float(budget_s) except: budget=2400
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget<60: budget=60
    total=max(60.0, float(budget)-35.0)
    deadline=start+total
    try: seed_int=int(seed)%1000000007+1 except: seed_int=12346
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed_int)
    target=1.499
    pool=[]
    parent=None
    try:
       parent_raw=globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    except Exception:
       parent_raw=None
    if parent_raw is not None:
       p=_sanitize(parent_raw)
       if p is not None:
          sc,_=_score_d(p)
          _add_candidate(pool,p,sc,64)
          parent=p
    if not pool:
       p=np.ones(3072,dtype=float)/3072.0
       _add_candidate(pool,p,None,64)
       parent=p
    # determine base_n
    base_n = int(parent.size) if parent is not None else 3072
    if base_n > 4096: base_n=4096
    elif base_n < 1024: base_n=2048
    # if base_n in [1024,8192] but not common, keep? Could be 3073; FFT okay. But _search_size with arbitrary n okay. To reduce sizes, snap to nearest common:
    common=[1024,2048,3072,4096]
    # if not in common, choose nearest but keep if within 10 of child? Simpler choose nearest common.
    bestdiff=10**9; bestc=3072
    for c in common:
       d=abs(base_n-c)
       if d<bestdiff: bestdiff=d; bestc=c
    base_n=bestc
    sizes=[base_n]
    if total>900 and 3072 not in sizes: sizes.append(3072)
    if total>600 and 2048 not in sizes and base_n != 2048: sizes.append(2048)
    if total>1700 and 4096 not in sizes: sizes.append(4096)
    # remove if too many? max 3 maybe 4.
    if len(sizes)>3:
       # keep base_n plus two smallest? If base=3072 had 3072,2048,4096 ->3 ok; if base=2048, 3072,4096 ->3. If base=1024, adds 3072,2048,4096=4; cap by dropping 4096? Let's sort? We'll just if len>3: del sizes[-1] maybe drops 4096. Fine.
    # fractions
    if len(sizes)==1: fracs=[1.0]
    elif len(sizes)==2: fracs=[0.7,0.3]
    else: fracs=[0.55,0.30,0.15]
    verbose = total > 600
    for idx,n in enumerate(sizes):
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem <= 60: break
       talloc=total*fracs[idx]
       # if already good, cap
       if len(pool)>0 and pool[0][0] < 1.505:
           talloc=min(talloc, max(30.0, 0.35*rem))
       talloc=min(max(0.0,talloc), max(0.0, rem-40.0))
       if talloc < 40: continue
       _search_size(parent,pool,n,talloc,rng,target,verbose)
    # final sort
    pool.sort(key=_item_key)
    if not pool:
       out=np.ones(3072)/3072.0
       return [float(x) for x in out]
    best_item=pool[0]
    selected=best_item[2].copy()
    # official check
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 15:
       for i in range(min(3,len(pool))):
          arr=pool[i][2]
          if arr.size <= 4096:
             try:
                lst=[float(x) for x in arr]
                sc=float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
             except Exception:
                sc=pool[i][0]
             if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < float(best_item[0]) - 1e-12:
                best_item=pool[i]; selected=pool[i][2].copy()
    out=np.asarray(selected,dtype=float).ravel()
    out=np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out=np.clip(out,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(np.sum(out))
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       out=np.ones(3072,dtype,float); return [float(x) for x in out]
    out=out/s
    # optional final clip tiny negative
    out=np.clip(out,0.0,1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(np.convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)**2.

The implementation is a hybrid local/global deterministic search:

1. Candidate pool:
   - mother sequence if provided by the environment,
   - several resampled versions of current best candidates,
   - random smooth/blocky/sigmoid shapes.

2. Fast exact scoring:
   - FFT-based full convolution for score,
   - FFT-based cross-correlation for directions.

3. Main descent:
   - active top-band of convolution coefficients,
   - two exact line-searched moves:
       * multiplicative move in log-space,
       * additive projected-gradient-style move on the simplex,
   - adaptive band, softmax beta, step sizes, and random restarts.

4. Polishing:
   - another narrowed descent pass,
   - random block scaling local search.

The program always returns the best sequence found before its time budget
expires.  The objective is homogeneous, so the returned sequence is
normalized to sum 1, but any nonnegative scaling would be harmless.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

_MIN_P = 1e-300
_FFT_INFO = {}


def _item_key(item):
    return float(item[0])


def _sanitize(p):
    """Convert input to normalized nonnegative finite float array, or None."""
    if p is None:
        return None
    try:
        a = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return a / s


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    y = 1
    while y < x:
        y <<= 1
    return y


def _fft_info(n):
    """FFT length and index map for the needed cross-correlation."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n in _FFT_INFO:
        return _FFT_INFO[n]
    need = 2 * n - 1
    L = _next_pow2(need)
    idx = np.empty(n, dtype=np.int64)
    idx[0] = 0
    if n > 1:
        idx[1:] = L - np.arange(1, n)
    _FFT_INFO[n] = (L, idx)
    return _FFT_INFO[n]


def _score_d(p):
    """Return exact score and scaled convolution d_j = n * c_j."""
    p = _sanitize(p)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf"), np.array([], dtype=float)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.array([], dtype=float)
    L, _ = _fft_info(n)
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fp, L)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    d = float(n) * c
    mx = float(np.max(d)) if d.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx < 0.0:
        return float("inf"), d
    return 2.0 * mx, d


def _corr_A(p, w):
    """
    A_i = sum_j w_j * p_{j-i}, for i=0..n-1,
    where p and w are zero-padded and w has length 2*n-1.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    L, idx = _fft_info(n)
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    circ = np.fft.irfft(Fp * np.conjugate(Fw), L)
    A = circ[idx]
    A = np.where(A > 0.0, A, 0.0)
    return A


def _softmax(z):
    """Stable softmax from log-unnormalized weights."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=30.0, neginf=-30.0)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        out = np.zeros(z.shape, dtype=float)
        out[int(np.argmax(z))] = 1.0
        return out
    return e / s


def _z_of_p(p):
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, _MIN_P))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    return z


def _resample(p, n):
    """Linearly resample a probability sequence to length n."""
    p = _sanitize(p)
    if p is None:
        return None
    n = int(max(1, n))
    m = int(p.size)
    if m == n:
        return p.copy()
    if m == 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float) / float(n)
    x_old = (np.arange(m, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(m)
    x_new = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0) + 1e-12
    return _sanitize(out)


def _make_weights(d, beta, tau):
    """Normalize nonnegative weights on coefficients, emphasizing d > tau."""
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=float).ravel()
    if d.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    maxd = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(maxd) or maxd <= 0.0:
        return np.full(d.shape, 1.0 / float(d.size), dtype=float)
    if not np.isfinite(tau):
        tau = 0.5 * maxd
    tau = float(tau)
    x = d - tau
    mask = x > 0.0
    if not np.any(mask):
        tau = max(0.0, maxd - max(1e-9, 0.05 * maxd))
        x = d - tau
        mask = x > 0.0
    if not np.any(mask):
        w = np.exp(beta * (d - maxd))
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            w = np.full(d.shape, 1.0 / float(d.size), dtype=float)
        else:
            w = w / s
        return w

    xm = float(np.max(x[mask]))
    w = np.zeros(d.shape, dtype=float)
    w[mask] = np.exp(float(beta) * (x[mask] - xm))
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.full(d.shape, 1.0 / float(d.size), dtype=float)
    else:
        w = w / s
    return w


def _random_p(n, style, rng):
    """Random positive shape, returned as normalized probability vector."""
    n = int(max(2, n))
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(n)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    style = int(style) % 4

    if style == 0:
        # Trigonometric asymmetry.
        z += rng.normal(0.0, 0.4)
        for k in range(1, 7):
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0) / max(1.0, float(k)))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * t + ph)
        z += float(rng.uniform(-1.5, 1.5)) * (t - 0.5)

    elif style == 1:
        # Smooth step/sigmoid shape.
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.75))
        s = float(rng.uniform(0.025, 0.25))
        a = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.8))
        b = float(rng.normal(2.0, 1.2))
        z = a + b / (1.0 + np.exp(-np.clip((t - c) / max(1e-6, s), -30.0, 30.0)))
        z += float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.6)) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (t - 0.5))

    elif style == 2:
        # Random piecewise-linear heights.
        max_k = max(5, min(48, int(n // 64) + 4))
        K = int(max(3, min(max_k, int(rng.integers(3, max_k + 1)))))
        edges = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K - 1))
        heights = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, K)
        z = np.interp(t, edges, heights).astype(float)
        z += float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.8)) * (t - 0.5)
        z += float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.4)) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t)

    else:
        # Smoothed white noise.
        wmax = max(3, int(n // 48))
        width = int(max(2, min(wmax, int(rng.integers(3, max(4, wmax + 1))))))
        k = np.arange(-width, width + 1, dtype=float)
        sig = max(1.0, width / 2.0)
        kern = np.exp(-0.5 * (k / sig) ** 2)
        kern = kern / float(kern.sum())
        z = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
        z = np.convolve(z, kern, mode="same")
        z += float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5)) * (t - 0.5)

    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=30.0, neginf=-30.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    return _softmax(z)


def _add_candidate(pool, p, score=None, keep=64):
    """Add candidate to priority list, keeping only best `keep`."""
    p = _sanitize(p)
    if p is None:
        return
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        score, _ = _score_d(p)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    pool.append((float(score), int(p.size), p.copy()))
    pool.sort(key=_item_key)
    if len(pool) > int(keep):
        del pool[int(keep):]


def _descent(p_init, n, time_limit, target_score, rng,
             band_start=0.15, beta_start=8.0, alpha_start=0.6,
             theta_start=0.6, verbose=False):
    """
    Local deterministic/adaptive search from one starting point.
    Uses exact score line search with multiplicative and additive moves.
    """
    p = _sanitize(p_init)
    if p is None or p.size != int(n):
        p = _resample(p_init, int(n))
    if p is None:
        p = np.ones(int(n), dtype=float) / float(int(n))
    n = int(max(1, n))
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    if p.size != n:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=float) / float(n)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=float) / float(n)
    p = p / s

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc, _ = _score_d(p)
    cur_sc = float(best_sc)

    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return best_p, best_sc

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(max(0.0, time_limit))
    inv_n = 1.0 / float(n)
    target_d = 0.5 * float(target_score)

    band = float(max(0.02, band_start))
    beta = float(max(1.0, beta_start))
    alpha = float(max(0.02, alpha_start))
    theta = float(max(0.02, theta_start))

    stall = 0
    hard_stall = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = -1000000

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cur_sc, d = _score_d(p)
        if not np.isfinite(cur_sc):
            p = best_p.copy()
            cur_sc = best_sc
            d, _ = _score_d(p)

        if np.isfinite(cur_sc) and cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_sc = float(cur_sc)
            best_p = p.copy()
            if verbose and (it - last_print >= 500):
                print(f"descent n={n} it={it} best={best_sc:.8f}")
                last_print = it

        maxd = float(np.max(d)) if d.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(maxd) or maxd <= 0.0:
            p = best_p.copy()
            cur_sc = best_sc
            continue

        # Band width in scaled coefficient units.
        b1 = max(0.02, band)
        b2 = 0.05 * maxd
        b3 = 0.35 * maxd if (maxd - target_d) > 0.35 else 0.0
        band_eff = max(b1, b2, b3)
        tau = max(0.0, maxd - band_eff)
        if tau >= maxd:
            tau = max(0.0, maxd - 1e-8 * maxd)

        w = _make_weights(d, beta, tau)
        A = _corr_A(p, w)
        na = float(n) * A
        mean_na = float(np.mean(na))
        B = float(np.dot(w, d))

        # Projected gradient in p-space.
        vp = na - mean_na
        # Log-space weighted gradient direction (without p_i factor).
        vl = na - B

        vpx = float(np.max(np.abs(vp)))
        if vpx < 1e-14:
            vp = vl
            vpx = float(np.max(np.abs(vp)))
        if vpx < 1e-14:
            # Directionally stuck: small random perturbation, then retry.
            z = _z_of_p(p) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, n)
            p = _softmax(z)
            cur_sc, _ = _score_d(p)
            stall += 1
            it += 1
            continue
        vp = vp / vpx

        vlx = float(np.max(np.abs(vl)))
        if vlx < 1e-14:
            vl = vp
        else:
            vl = vl / vlx

        improved = False

        # Multiplicative/log-space candidate.
        z = _z_of_p(p) - alpha * vl
        z = z - float(np.mean(z))
        pcand = _softmax(z)
        scand, _ = _score_d(pcand)
        if np.isfinite(scand) and scand < cur_sc - 1e-13:
            p = pcand
            cur_sc = float(scand)
            alpha = min(8.0, alpha * 1.18)
            improved = True

        # Additive simplex-like candidate.
        if not improved:
            q = p - theta * inv_n * vp
            q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
            q = np.clip(q, 0.0, 1000.0)
            qs = float(q.sum())
            if qs > 0.0 and np.isfinite(qs):
                q = q / qs
                scand, _ = _score_d(q)
                if np.isfinite(scand) and scand < cur_sc - 1e-13:
                    p = q
                    cur_sc = float(scand)
                    theta = min(8.0, theta * 1.18)
                    improved = True

        if improved:
            stall = 0
            hard_stall = 0
            band = max(0.025, band * 0.965)
            beta = min(160.0, beta * 1.012)
        else:
            stall += 1
            alpha = max(0.03, alpha * 0.62)
            theta = max(0.03, theta * 0.62)
            band = min(0.45, band * 1.18)
            beta = max(1.0, beta * 0.95)

            if stall >= 7:
                sigma = 0.05 + 0.008 * float(min(stall, 50))
                z = _z_of_p(p) + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
                pcand = _softmax(z)
                scand, _ = _score_d(pcand)
                accept = (
                    np.isfinite(scand)
                    and scand <= max(best_sc * 1.30, cur_sc * 1.40)
                    and scand < 3.0
                )
                if accept:
                    p = pcand
                    cur_sc = float(scand)
                else:
                    p = best_p.copy()
                    cur_sc = float(best_sc)
                    hard_stall += 1

                stall = 0
                alpha = max(0.20, alpha)
                theta = max(0.20, theta)
                band = max(0.08, band * 1.10)
                beta = min(80.0, beta * 1.10)

                if hard_stall >= 4:
                    pcand = _random_p(n, it % 4, rng)
                    scand, _ = _score_d(pcand)
                    if np.isfinite(scand) and scand < 2.5:
                        p = pcand
                        cur_sc = float(scand)
                    else:
                        p = best_p.copy()
                        cur_sc = float(best_sc)
                    hard_stall = 0
                    band = max(0.18, band)
                    beta = 6.0
                    alpha = 0.7
                    theta = 0.7

        if verbose and (it - last_print >= 2000):
            print(f"descent n={n} it={it} cur={cur_sc:.8f} best={best_sc:.8f}")
            last_print = it

        it += 1

    return best_p, float(best_sc)


def _local_block_search(p_init, time_limit, rng, verbose=False):
    """Polish a sequence by exact line searches over random block scales."""
    p = _sanitize(p_init)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return p, float("inf")

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc, _ = _score_d(p)
    cur_sc = float(best_sc)

    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return best_p, best_sc

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(max(0.0, time_limit))
    stalled = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = -1000000

    small = max(1, n // 2048)
    mid = max(small + 1, n // 256)
    large = max(mid + 1, min(n, n // 32))
    factors = (0.55, 0.75, 0.88, 1.12, 1.30, 1.65)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        r = float(rng.random())
        if n <= 64:
            w = int(max(1, min(n, int(rng.integers(1, n + 1)))))
        elif r < 0.35:
            w = 1
        elif r < 0.70:
            hi = max(2, small * 8 + 1)
            w = int(max(1, min(n, int(rng.integers(1, hi + 1)))))
        elif r < 0.95:
            lo = max(1, mid)
            hi = max(lo + 1, mid * 10 + 1)
            w = int(max(1, min(n, int(rng.integers(lo, min(n, hi) + 1)))))
        else:
            lo = max(1, large)
            hi = max(lo + 1, large * 10 + 1)
            w = int(max(1, min(n, int(rng.integers(lo, min(n, hi) + 1)))))

        if w >= n:
            start = 0
        else:
            start = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))

        local_best = None
        local_best_sc = cur_sc

        for f in factors:
            cand = p.copy()
            cand[start:start + w] *= float(f)
            cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
            cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
            cs = float(cand.sum())
            if cs <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cs):
                continue
            cand = cand / cs
            sc, _ = _score_d(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_sc - 1e-13:
                local_best_sc = float(sc)
                local_best = cand

        if local_best is not None:
            p = local_best
            cur_sc = local_best_sc
            stalled = 0
            if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_sc = cur_sc
                best_p = p.copy()
                if verbose and (it - last_print >= 500):
                    print(f"block n={n} it={it} best={best_sc:.8f}")
                    last_print = it
        else:
            stalled += 1
            if stalled >= 18:
                z = _z_of_p(p) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, n)
                cand = _softmax(z)
                sc, _ = _score_d(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= max(best_sc * 1.08, cur_sc * 1.18):
                    p = cand
                    cur_sc = float(sc)
                else:
                    p = best_p.copy()
                    cur_sc = float(best_sc)
                stalled = 0

            if stalled >= 150:
                p = best_p.copy()
                cur_sc = float(best_sc)
                stalled = 0

        it += 1

    return best_p, float(best_sc)


def _search_size(parent_p, pool, n, t_alloc, rng, target_score, verbose=False):
    """Run a whole search for one sequence length n."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    n = int(max(2, n))
    t_alloc = float(max(0.0, t_alloc))
    if t_alloc < 25.0:
        return

    local_p = None
    local_sc = float("inf")
    good_thresh = float(target_score) + 0.025

    starts = []

    if parent_p is not None:
        pp = _resample(parent_p, n)
        if pp is not None:
            starts.append((pp, 0.16, 8.0, 0.7, 0.7))

    if len(pool) > 0:
        bi = 0
        for i in range(1, len(pool)):
            if _item_key(pool[i]) < _item_key(pool[bi]):
                bi = i
        gp = _resample(pool[bi][2], n)
        if gp is not None:
            starts.append((gp, 0.09, 30.0, 0.35, 0.35))

    if len(pool) >= 2:
        order = list(range(len(pool)))
        # insertion sort by score to avoid lambda
        for i in range(1, len(order)):
            key = order[i]
            j = i - 1
            while j >= 0 and _item_key(pool[order[j]]) > _item_key(pool[key]):
                order[j + 1] = order[j]
                j -= 1
            order[j + 1] = key
        p1 = _resample(pool[order[0]][2], n)
        p2 = _resample(pool[order[1]][2], n)
        if p1 is not None and p2 is not None:
            cross = _sanitize(0.5 * p1 + 0.5 * p2)
            if cross is not None:
                starts.append((cross, 0.20, 8.0, 0.55, 0.55))

    starts.append((_random_p(n, 1, rng), 0.26, 5.0, 0.90, 0.90))
    if t_alloc > 700.0:
        starts.append((_random_p(n, 2, rng), 0.32, 4.0, 1.00, 1.00))

    max_starts = 3 if t_alloc > 900.0 else (2 if t_alloc > 350.0 else 1)
    if len(starts) > max_starts:
        starts = starts[:max_starts]

    if len(starts) == 1:
        fracs = [1.0]
    elif len(starts) == 2:
        fracs = [0.60, 0.40]
    else:
        fracs = [0.55, 0.30, 0.15]

    run_frac = 0.62 if t_alloc > 220.0 else 0.90
    total_run = min(t_alloc * run_frac, max(0.0, t_alloc - 30.0))

    for j in range(len(starts)):
        rem = t_alloc - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if rem <= 30.0:
            break
        alloc = total_run * fracs[j]
        t = min(float(alloc), max(0.0, rem - 30.0))
        if np.isfinite(local_sc) and local_sc < good_thresh:
            t = min(t, max(20.0, 0.18 * rem))
        if t < 15.0:
            continue

        p0, band0, beta0, alpha0, theta0 = starts[j]
        p1, sc1 = _descent(
            p0, n, t, target_score, rng,
            band_start=band0, beta_start=beta0,
            alpha_start=alpha0, theta_start=theta0,
            verbose=verbose
        )
        if p1 is not None and np.isfinite(sc1):
            _add_candidate(pool, p1, sc1, 80)
            if sc1 < local_sc:
                local_sc = float(sc1)
                local_p = p1.copy()

    if local_p is None:
        local_p = _random_p(n, 0, rng)
        local_sc, _ = _score_d(local_p)
        _add_candidate(pool, local_p, local_sc, 80)

    # Narrowed polish descent.
    rem = t_alloc - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 70.0:
        t = min(rem - 60.0, max(30.0, 0.16 * t_alloc))
        band_polish = max(0.035, min(0.09, 0.035 * float(local_sc)))
        if np.isfinite(local_sc) and local_sc > 1.05:
            t = max(t, min(rem - 50.0, 0.22 * t_alloc))
        p1, sc1 = _descent(
            local_p, n, t, target_score, rng,
            band_start=band_polish, beta_start=30.0,
            alpha_start=0.25, theta_start=0.35,
            verbose=verbose
        )
        if p1 is not None and np.isfinite(sc1):
            _add_candidate(pool, p1, sc1, 80)
            if sc1 < local_sc:
                local_sc = float(sc1)
                local_p = p1.copy()

    # Block local search.
    rem = t_alloc - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 50.0:
        if np.isfinite(local_sc) and local_sc < float(target_score) + 0.12:
            bt = min(rem - 20.0, max(60.0, 0.34 * t_alloc))
        else:
            bt = min(rem - 20.0, max(30.0, 0.24 * t_alloc))
        p1, sc1 = _local_block_search(local_p, bt, rng, verbose=verbose)
        if p1 is not None and np.isfinite(sc1):
            _add_candidate(pool, p1, sc1, 80)
            if sc1 < local_sc:
                local_sc = float(sc1)
                local_p = p1.copy()


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, seed=12345):
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget < 60.0:
        budget = 60.0

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    total = max(60.0, float(budget) - 35.0)
    deadline = start_time + total

    try:
        seed_int = int(seed) % 1000000007 + 1
    except Exception:
        seed_int = 123456789
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed_int)

    target_score = 1.499

    pool = []
    parent = None

    try:
        raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    except Exception:
        raw = None

    if raw is not None:
        p = _sanitize(raw)
        if p is not None:
            sc, _ = _score_d(p)
            _add_candidate(pool, p, sc, 80)
            parent = p

    if not pool:
        p = np.ones(3072, dtype=float) / 3072.0
        _add_candidate(pool, p, None, 80)
        parent = p

    # Choose search lengths.  Keep the problem sufficiently fast while
    # allowing a small amount of larger / smaller attack surface.
    base_n = int(parent.size) if parent is not None else 3072
    if base_n > 4096:
        base_n = 4096
    elif base_n < 1024:
        base_n = 2048

    common = (1024, 2048, 3072, 4096)
    best_c = 3072
    best_diff = 10**18
    for c in common:
        d = abs(base_n - c)
        if d < best_diff:
            best_diff = d
            best_c = c
    base_n = int(best_c)

    sizes = [base_n]
    if total > 900.0 and 3072 not in sizes:
        sizes.append(3072)
    if total > 600.0 and 2048 not in sizes:
        sizes.append(2048)
    if total > 1700.0 and 4096 not in sizes:
        sizes.append(4096)

    if len(sizes) > 3:
        # Preserve base_n and drop the last extra.
        sizes = sizes[:3]

    if len(sizes) == 1:
        fracs = [1.0]
    elif len(sizes) == 2:
        fracs = [0.70, 0.30]
    else:
        fracs = [0.55, 0.30, 0.15]

    verbose = total > 900.0

    for idx, n in enumerate(sizes):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem <= 60.0:
            break
        talloc = total * fracs[idx]
        if len(pool) > 0 and _item_key(pool[0]) < 1.506:
            talloc = min(talloc, max(30.0, 0.35 * rem))
        talloc = min(max(0.0, talloc), max(0.0, rem - 40.0))
        if talloc < 40.0:
            continue
        _search_size(parent, pool, int(n), float(talloc), rng, target_score, verbose=verbose)

    pool.sort(key=_item_key)

    if not pool:
        out = np.ones(3072, dtype=float)
        return [float(x) for x in out]

    best_item = pool[0]
    selected = best_item[2].copy()

    # Optional official evaluator verification on small top candidates.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 12.0:
        for i in range(min(3, len(pool))):
            arr = pool[i][2]
            if arr.size <= 4096:
                try:
                    lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
                    official = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
                except Exception:
                    official = float(pool[i][0])
                if np.isfinite(official) and official < float(pool[i][0]) - 1e-12:
                    pool[i] = (official, int(arr.size), arr.copy())
                if np.isfinite(official) and official < float(best_item[0]) - 1e-12:
                    best_item = (official, int(arr.size), arr.copy())
                    selected = arr.copy()
        pool.sort(key=_item_key)

    out = np.asarray(selected, dtype=float).ravel()
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        out = np.ones(3072, dtype=float)
    else:
        out = out / s
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
